Empty or over-limit PCM frames are rejected fail-closed. pcm_samples Input parameter. Return value. Calls: empty(), THEMIS_WARN(), size(), insert(), end(), begin(), runCallStt(), on_transcript().

#### `CallTranscript receiveRtpPacket(const std::vector< uint8_t > &rtp_packet)`
- Source: `include/voice/voice_telephony.h`:240
- Brief: Feed an RTP packet from the network.
- Parameters:
  - `rtp_packet` (const std::vector< uint8_t > &): Input parameter.
- Return: Incremental CallTranscript, or empty if no new hypothesis.
- Details: Receive Rtp Packet. Strips the RTP header, decodes the payload with the negotiated codec, appends PCM to the STT pipeline, and returns any incremental transcript. rtp_packet Raw RTP packet bytes (header + payload). Incremental CallTranscript, or empty if no new hypothesis. Malformed RTP headers, empty payloads, and misaligned linear-audio payloads are rejected fail-closed. rtp_packet Input parameter. Return value. Calls: empty(), THEMIS_WARN(), on_error(), telephonyNowMs(), end(), size(), isRtpVersion2(), stripRtpHeader().

#### `AudioConvertFn makeFFmpegAudioConvertFn()`
- Source: `include/voice/voice_assistant.h`:169
- Brief: Create a libavformat/libavcodec (FFmpeg) backed AudioConvertFn.
- Parameters: none
- Return: A ready-to-use AudioConvertFn or an empty function when FFmpeg is unavailable.
- Details: ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── Factory: FFmpeg-backed AudioConvertFn Guarded by THEMIS_VOICE_HAS_FFMPEG (set when THEMIS_HAS_FFMPEG or THEMIS_ENABLE_FFMPEG is defined and libavformat/libavcodec are linked). When built with THEMIS_HAS_FFMPEG defined (libavformat + libavcodec linked), returns a concrete AudioConvertFn that transcodes audio using FFmpeg's libavformat demuxer and libavcodec encoder/decoder pipeline. The returned fn is safe to call from multiple threads (no shared state). When built without FFmpeg, returns an empty std::function so that convertAudioFormat() falls through to the passthrough fallback. Usage at startup: assistant.setAudioConvertFn(VoiceAssistant::makeFFmpegAudioConvertFn()); A ready-to-use AudioConvertFn or an empty function when FFmpeg is unavailable. Return value. Returns an empty function when FFmpeg is unavailable. ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

#### `LivenessScore detect_liveness(const std::vector< uint8_t > &audio_sample)`
- Source: `include/voice/voice_auth.h`:244
- Brief: Detect liveness: distinguish live speech from replay/synthesis.
- Parameters:
  - `audio_sample` (const std::vector< uint8_t > &): Input parameter.
- Return: LivenessScore with is_live==true when the sample appears genuine.
- Details: Detect liveness. Uses fail-closed heuristics for clipped, low-variability, and replay-like PCM patterns before combining crest factor and spectral flatness signals. Analyzes acoustic properties to detect: Replay attacks (same audio repeated) Synthetic/TTS speech Extreme audio conditions Silence or noise audio_data Raw audio samples audio_size Size of audio in bytes sample_rate Sample rate in Hz LivenessCheckResult with detection outcome std::invalid_argument if parameters are invalid audio_data Input parameter. audio_size Input parameter. sample_rate Input parameter. Return value. std::invalid_argument if an error occurs. Calls: is_silence_or_noise_only(), estimate_liveness_confidence(), detect_spoof_indicators(), compute_audio_hash(), is_replay_detected(), std::max(), uint8_t().

#### `std::string processOffer(const std::string &sdp_offer)`
- Source: `include/voice/voice_telephony.h`:373
- Brief: Process an SDP offer from the remote peer.
- Parameters:
  - `sdp_offer` (const std::string &): Input parameter.
- Return: SDP answer string to be returned to the peer.
- Throws:
  - std::runtime_error: if SDP cannot be parsed or no codec matches.
  - std::runtime_error: if an error occurs.
- Details: Process Offer. Selects the best matching codec from the offer, generates an SDP answer, and prepares ICE/DTLS parameters. sdp_offer SDP string from the remote WebRTC client. SDP answer string to be returned to the peer. std::runtime_error if SDP cannot be parsed or no codec matches. sdp_offer Input parameter. Return value. std::runtime_error if an error occurs. Calls: empty(), parseSdpCodec(), generateCallId(), buildSdpAnswer(), setState(), on_local_ice(), str(), THEMIS_INFO().

#### `CallTranscript receiveAudioFrame(const std::vector< int16_t > &pcm_samples)`
- Source: `include/voice/voice_telephony.h`:410
- Brief: Feed a decoded PCM audio frame from the DTLS-SRTP stack.
- Parameters:
  - `pcm_samples` (const std::vector< int16_t > &): Input parameter.
- Return: Incremental CallTranscript.
- Details: Receive Audio Frame. pcm_samples 16-bit signed PCM at the negotiated sample rate. Incremental CallTranscript. pcm_samples Input parameter. Return value. Calls: telephonyNowMs(), THEMIS_WARN(), end(), insert(), begin(), size(), runCallStt(), on_transcript().

#### `void setTtsBackend(std::shared_ptr< ITtsBackend > backend)`
- Source: `include/voice/voice_telephony.h`:437
- Brief: Inject a TTS backend for real Opus audio synthesis.
- Parameters:
  - `backend` (std::shared_ptr< ITtsBackend >): Input parameter.
- Details: Set Tts Backend. When set, synthesizeTts() delegates to this backend. Pass nullptr to revert to the stub (raw-text) fallback. Thread safety: must be called before the first synthesizeTts() call. backend Input parameter. Calls: std::move().

- Source: `include/voice/voice_telephony.h`:427
- Brief: Synthesise text and return Opus-encoded RTP packets.
- Parameters:
  - `text` (const std::string &): n/a
- Details: When a TTS backend has been injected via setTtsBackend(), delegates synthesis to that backend and wraps each returned frame with a minimal Opus RTP header (PT=111). Without an injected backend the previous stub behaviour (raw text bytes in an RTP packet) is retained for compatibility.
